Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on np structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 21. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 87 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to np structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 21/87 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the np structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 153. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 668 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on np structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 153. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 668. QED.
